Sessions

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

Session 0: Introduction to the course: setting the scene • Training course materials: o facilitator guide, participant manual o PCPNC manual o national ANC protocols and guidelines o reference documents (MCPC manual, RCP, WHO recommendations for a positive pregnancy experience 2016) • Laptop computer

### Responsibility Name Of Facilitator (S) — phần 3

Preparation • Gather training materials in advance

Presentation of the session:

Slide 1: Title slide Present this slide to welcome participants to the course

Slide 2: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience Every year, women become pregnant and hope for a positive outcome for themselves and their unborn babies. In most cases, women will have a normal pregnancy and can hope for childbirth without any complications. As antenatal care providers, we have the opportunity to work with the woman and her family to promote a healthy, normal pregnancy and, where needed, to identify and manage any complications or problems that may occur.

Slide 3: What are pregnant women dying from? Ask participants to identify the major causes of maternal deaths shown in the chart. Ask if these causes are preventable and if antenatal care can contribute substantially to reducing these deaths.

### Responsibility Name Of Facilitator (S) — phần 4

Ask why these causes continue to be major contributors to maternal death even though in many countries ANC coverage is high and increasing?

List the major causes of maternal death emphasizing their magnitudes and the fact that they are largely preventable. Reasons why they continue to be major killers include: poor quality of care provided, late booking with few contact visits, and low levels of supervised deliveries.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 1

Slide 4: Emphasize benefits of quality ANC. The availability of routine antenatal care, including antenatal screening and diagnosis, plays a major part in reducing maternal death rates and miscarriages as well as birth defects, low birth weight, neonatal infections and other preventable health problems.

### Responsibility Name Of Facilitator (S) — phần 5

The high coverage of ANC and repeated contacts between the woman and the health services offer many opportunities for providing evidence-based interventions related to health promotion, disease prevention, screening, and treatment likely to affect maternal, foetal, and neonatal health and survival.

ANC is also an entry point for the provision of integrated care as it provides a platform for multiple interventions and programmes such as prevention of sexually transmitted illnesses and mother-to-child- transmission of HIV/hepatitis B/syphilis; prevention and case management of malaria in pregnancy; prevention of maternal and neonatal tetanus; prevention of maternal anaemia and malnutrition, etc.

Slide 5: Providing quality ANC is critical High antenatal coverage rates on the African continent have not resulted in the desired reductions in maternal and foetal mortality and morbidity.

### Responsibility Name Of Facilitator (S) — phần 6

Link this fact to the issues of poor-quality antenatal care, emphasizing that quality ANC is imperative and must be universally available.

Slide 6: Benefits of quality ANC Quality ANC is an opportunity for the health provider to establish partnerships with pregnant women and their families that will endure beyond the pregnancy.

It is important to provide quality ANC services to encourage pregnant women and their families to use services during pregnancy, labour and childbirth, the postpartum period, and beyond.

Contacts with pregnant women also offer opportunities to promote healthy pregnancies, educate women and their families to make informed choices about their care, and support them to prepare for childbirth and care of the newborn.

### Responsibility Name Of Facilitator (S) — phần 7

Slide 7: Women want a positive pregnancy experience during ANC. Note that these are the findings from a systematic review of women’s views. A positive pregnancy experience is defined as maintaining physical and sociocultural normality, maintaining a healthy pregnancy for mother and baby (including preventing or treating risks, illness and death), having an effective transition to positive labour and birth, and achieving positive motherhood

(including maternal self-esteem, competence and autonomy).

## 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

The concept of “antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience”

Slide 8: The new ANC model for a positive pregnancy experience The existing ANC model of care is being revised to achieve the goals of creating a positive pregnancy experience for women.

Discuss the listed care components areas in the slide that are being strengthened in the new ANC care model.

Slide 9: Why the WHO recommendations on ANC ? Present the evidence informing the new guideline as listed in the slide. You may ask a participant to read the slide aloud.

Refer participants to the relevant references in the manual. “WHO recommendations on antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience”

Slide 10: The WHO ANC model • Briefly present the eight-contact schedule in the new model and inform participants that this schedule will be further discussed in Session 3: Providing antenatal care.

### 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evidence also shows that women want flexible appointment systems and

continuity of provider care where they are given privacy and time to build authentic and supportive relationships with maternity care providers (high confidence in the evidence).

Slide 11: Early ultrasound for all pregnant women • This recommendation was based on evidence showing the benefits of a single ultrasound done early (before 24 weeks) on maternal and foetal outcomes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The evidence was derived from a Cochrane review of 11 RCTs conducted in

Australia, Norway, South Africa, Sweden, the United Kingdom and the USA, involving 37 505 women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; An ongoing multi-country trial in low-resource settings is under way and

will contribute further evidence on health effects, care utilization and implementation-related information on ultrasound in these settings.

### 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

Slide 12: A health systems approach to ANC • The new ANC model highlights health system changes that are required to create a positive care environment for care throughout pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; It includes integrated care, good client–provider communication, continuity

of care and creating ideal ANC clinic settings.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 3

Introducing the training course

Slide 13: Overarching aim of the ANC training course The overarching aim of antenatal care is to provide pregnant women with respectful, individualized, person-centred care at every contact, with implementation of effective clinical practices (interventions and tests), and provision of relevant and timely information, and psychosocial and emotional support, by practitioners with good clinical and interpersonal skills within a well-functioning health system.

### 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

Slide 14: The goal of this ANC training course • The goal of this training course is to strengthen the capacity of ANC service providers to provide their clients with quality antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience. • This course is intended for use in both pre-service and in-service training of ANC providers.

Slide 15: Course objectives The objectives of the training course are to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; build the capacity of ANC providers in the organization of, and systems-based

approach to, ANC services;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; increase ANC providers’ knowledge and capacity to provide evidence-based

practices for quality antenatal care;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; enhance the ability of ANC providers to provide appropriate counselling and

health education messages based on an improved awareness of the cultural and social environments of the pregnant women who seek care;

### 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; improve health care providers’ attitudes and capacity to provide quality

client-centred antenatal care; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; strengthen the interpersonal and communication skills of health care providers

to meet the emotional, physical and support needs of ANC clients.

Structure of the training course

Slides 16 and 17: Structure of the training course This training course deals with antenatal care, including the common clinical conditions associated with the antenatal period. It includes 14 sessions plus a post-training follow-up session:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 0:; Introduction to the course

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 1:; Understanding antenatal care

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 2:; Organizing antenatal care services

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 3:; Providing antenatal care for a positive pregnancy

experience

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 4:; Identifying antenatal problems

### 2 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 5:; Managing antenatal problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 6:; Providing preventive care measures

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 7:; Antenatal counselling

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 8:; Developing a birth and emergency plan

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 9:; Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy

## 4 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 4 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 10:; Prevention and management of anaemia in

pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 11:; Preventing mother-to-child transmission of HIV,

syphilis and hepatitis B

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 12:; Ultrasonography in antenatal care

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 13:; Skills practice: selected clinical skills

- Dòng dữ liệu: Session 14:; Post-course follow-up and support for ANC

implementation

The estimated duration of the full training course is five days. However, the actual time frame will depend on factors such as the learning needs and abilities of participants and the resources available to support the teaching–learning process

## Present The Course Schedule:

### Present The Course Schedule: — phần 1

Go through the course schedule (agenda)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 5

Introduction to the course materials

Slide 18: Course materials and reference documents • Read the slides and present the course materials and reference documents. • Distribute the course materials, including reference documents. • Explain to participants how they will use the different materials during the training.

Introduction to the PCPNC guide

A primary reference for this course is the WHO document titled Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care: a guide for essential practice (hereinafter the PCPNC guide), which provides evidence-based standards for the provision of high-quality pregnancy and childbirth care.

### Present The Course Schedule: — phần 2

Explain the purpose and structure of the manual and how it is used. Answer any questions the participants may have using the information provided below.

Using the PCPNC guide

The PCPNC is a guide for clinical decision-making. It facilitates the collection, analysis, classification and use of relevant clinical information by suggesting key questions, observations and/or examinations to perform, and then recommends appropriate evidence-based interventions.

Help trainees familiarize themselves with the clinical flow chart frameworks in the PCPNC guide.

### Present The Course Schedule: — phần 3

The PCPNC content is presented in a series of coloured flow charts supported by treatment and information charts which give further details of care. These flow charts include guidance for routine care, early detection and management of complications, preventive measures, and advice and counselling. The framework of the charts is based on a syndromic approach whereby the skilled attendant identifies a limited number of key clinical signs and

symptoms, enabling him / her to classify the condition according to severity and give appropriate treatment.

Flow charts in the PCPNC include the following information: (see also chart below)

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; Key questions to be asked

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; Important observations and examinations to conduct

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.; Possible findings (signs) based on the information gathered from the questions, observations and examinations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4.; Classification of the findings

### Present The Course Schedule: — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: 5.; Treatments and advice related to the signs and classifications

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1; 2; 3; 4; 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: Ask, check; Look, listen,; Treat and

- Dòng dữ liệu: Signs; Classify

- Dòng dữ liệu: record; feel; advise

## 6 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 6 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Use of colour in the flow charts: the severity of a condition is marked in colour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: 6.; Green usually indicates no abnormal condition and therefore normal care is given (including appropriate

advice for home care and follow-up).

- Dòng dữ liệu: 7.; Yellow indicates that there is a problem that can be treated without referral.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 8.; Red highlights an emergency which requires immediate treatment and, in most cases, also urgent referral

to a higher level facility.

In the flow charts, “treat, advise” means giving the treatment indicated (performing a procedure, prescribing drugs or other treatments, advising on possible side-effects and how to overcome them) and giving advice on other important practices. The treat and advice column is often cross-referenced to other treatment and/or information charts.

Treatment and information pages

### 6 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The flow charts are often linked (cross-referenced) to relevant treatment and/or information pages in other

parts of the guide.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; These pages include information which is usually too detailed to include in the flow charts and listed under such

headings as “Treatments”, “Advice and counselling”, “Preventive measures” and “Relevant procedures”.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The PCPNC guide also contains individual sheets with simplified messages on care during pregnancy and

childbirth, to be given to the pregnant woman, her partner and family. These can be accessed for use during client counselling.

Remind participants that the PCPNC manual will be referred to and used for the management of identified problems during antenatal care and client counseling. Encourage them to practice and familiarize themselves with the use of the manual

### 6 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 7

Session 1: Understanding antenatal care • Reference manuals: PCPNC guide; WHO ANC recommendations (2016)

Preparation • Gather training materials in advance • Read through the content of this course, including the information in the participant manual.

Introduction and welcome

Slide 1: Welcome and introduce participants to the Session. • Ask participants to open Session 1 of their training manual. • Using slide 2, describe the outline of the Session.

Slide 3: Goal and objectives • Read and explain the goal and objectives of the Session. • Provide participants with information on the historical background of antenatal care using the information provided below. • Ask participants what they know about the history of ANC in their respective counties. How has it evolved over the years?

Historical background of ANC

### 6 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

Specialized care for pregnant women through a public health care system started in the United Kingdom of Great Britain and Northern Ireland in the late 1930s. Formalized ANC was primarily initiated to address deaths due to eclampsia which had remained high. Deaths from sepsis, obstructed labour and haemorrhage had all been reduced by the institutionalized deliveries system. The new ANC practice was subsequently adopted by many other countries during the second half of the

20th century. Developing countries also adopted these ANC practices with little or no modifications after colonization and independence.

### 6 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

Institutionalized ANC is considered one of the most successful maternal health programmes in terms of its acceptance and patronage. Worldwide, over 70% of pregnant women have had at least one ANC visit (developed (98%), developing (68%)). Indeed, providing access to ANC for women has been easy to implement in many developing settings and has become the platform for the implementation of many other programmes, such as PMTCT and malaria prevention and control.

## 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

In recent years, practitioners have questioned the scientific basis and benefits of many of the practices of “traditional” ANC, including number of visits and risk categorization, as these did not appear to significantly reduce the high maternal mortality ratios in many countries. In fact, some approaches have been associated with increased perinatal mortality.

Evidence from recent randomized controlled trials and systematic reviews have shown that a newer model of ANC based on eight high-quality contacts yields optimal pregnancy outcomes for mother and baby. This new evidence-based ANC approach is what is being advocated in this training.

Defining antenatal care (10 minutes)

Ask the participants to define antenatal care. Write their responses on the blackboard or on a flip chart. Then present Slides 4 and 5 and compare their answers with the definition provided. Discuss any discrepancies or gaps in participants’ knowledge.

### 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Slides 4 and 5: Definition of antenatal care: • Enrich the discussions on the definition by providing the following additional information if necessary: • Antenatal care is preventive health care provided to a pregnant woman with the goal of ensuring regular check-ups that allow health providers to treat and prevent potential health problems throughout the course of the pregnancy, while promoting healthy lifestyles that benefit both mother and child.

• Antenatal care should focus on both the medical and psychosocial needs of each pregnant woman, within the context of the health care system and the sociocultural environment in which the woman lives. Periodic visits to a health care provider during pregnancy are necessary for the following reasons: o early detection and treatment of complications o communication and establishment of a supportive relationship between the woman and the health care provider

### 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

o development of a birth and emergency plan with the woman o provision of preventive measures, and o provision of advice and counselling based on the woman’s individual needs and circumstances.

Why is antenatal care important? (5 minutes)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the participants: why is antenatal care is important? Listen actively to their answers.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When there are no more contributions, ask the participants to read in turn (a paragraph at a time),

from the participant manual, the section “Why is antenatal care important?”

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Also refer to and use the information below to reinforce correct answers.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 9

Slides 6 and 7: Why ANC is important The SDGs have set ambitious health-related targets for mothers, newborns and children under the umbrella of universal health coverage by 2030.

### 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

ANC is an entry point for the provision of integrated care as it provides a platform for evidence-based interventions capable of affecting maternal, foetal and neonatal health and survival, such as prevention of mother-to-child transmission of HIV, prevention and case management of malaria in pregnancy, prevention of maternal and neonatal tetanus, and prevention of maternal anaemia and malnutrition.

Antenatal care is also a pillar of safe-motherhood and a key component in the continuum of maternal and newborn health care (from pre-pregnancy through pregnancy, childbirth and the postpartum period).

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience (40 minutes)

### 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

Slide 8: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience Read out aloud or explain what antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience is: • The WHO recommendations on ANC for a positive pregnancy experience highlight that a woman’s contact with her provider should be more than a simple visit. It should be an opportunity for comprehensive, high quality care, including medical care, support, and the provision of timely and relevant information throughout pregnancy.

• Depending on the context of the country, the definition of contact may include scheduled ANC visits and information sessions for pregnant women with relevant caretakers at the household, community and health facility levels.

Slide 9: Elements of a positive pregnancy experience Explain each of the listed elements and what has changed from the FANC approach.

### 8 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC • Present the guiding principles listed on the slide • Discuss why these five principles are important for a positive pregnancy ex- perience • Refer to the text below in facilitating discussions

## 10 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 10 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A unique feature of care during pregnancy is that a long-term relationship can be established

between the care provider and a client.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For many women, pregnancy is what first brings them in contact with the formal health care

system.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Their perceptions of the quality of care and their relationship with the care provider can have

long-term effects on whether they come to a health facility for childbirth and their future use of the health care system.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Negative experiences reported by other ANC clients may also have a negative impact on

utilization of ANC services by others.

Quality of antenatal care

Slide 11: Definition of quality of care Ask a participant to read the definition of quality of care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; WHO defines quality of care as “the extent to which health care services

### 10 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

provided to individuals and patient populations improve the likelihood of desired health outcomes”. To achieve this, health care must be safe, effective, timely, efficient, equitable and people-centred.

Explain the two aspects of QOC – provision and experience of care using the information below:

The health systems approach provides the structure for quality improvement in the two linked dimensions of provision and experience of care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provision of care: includes the use of evidence-based practices for routine and emergency care;

information systems in which recordkeeping allows review and auditing; and functioning systems for referral between different levels of care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Experience of care: consists of effective communication with women and their families about

### 10 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

the care provided, including their expectations and rights; care with respect and preservation of dignity; and access to the social and emotional support of their choice.

Other prerequisites for good quality of care include the availability of competent, motivated human resources and physical resources in health facilities.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 11

Characteristics of quality care

Slides 12 and 13: Characteristics of quality of care Present these two slides which describe the characteristics of quality care – linking them to ANC provision. Give examples to highlight the different characteristics: • Safe: delivering health care that minimizes risks and harm to service users, including avoiding preventable injuries and reducing medical errors. • Effective: providing services based on scientific knowledge and evidence-based guidelines.

### 10 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

• Timely: reducing delays in providing and receiving health care. Empha- size the need to negotiate with client for the best time to attend ANC in view of their other activities, including time for specialized services such as ultrasound. • Efficient: delivering health care in a manner that maximizes resource use and avoids waste. • Equitable: delivering health care that does not differ in quality based on personal characteristics such as gender, race, ethnicity, geographical

location or socioeconomic status. • People-centred – providing care that takes into account the preferences and aspirations of individual service users and the culture of their community.

Add aspects of experience of care to this discussion, including health worker attitudes and respectful maternal care.

Individualized and person-centred care at every contact

### 10 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

Slide 14: What is person-centred care? Ask two or three participants to describe what person-centred antenatal care means to them. Write their answers on the flip chart. Present slide 14 and explain that: • Individualized/ person-centred ANC refers to care that is acceptable to the pregnant woman and her family. • During person-centred care, the pregnant woman’s health and survival, basic human rights and comforts are given clear priority. The woman’s

personal desires and preferences are also regarded as important.

Slide 15: Individualized person-centred care Individualized care means that women are provided with person-centred care that meets their personal needs, medical condition, as well as cultural and socioeconomic circumstances.

Individualized care depends on good rapport and communication between the care provider and the pregnant woman.

You may use the additional information provided below to further buttress your points.

## 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Person-centred antenatal care • Her culture, beliefs, traditions as well as gender roles and relations are respected. • Family members are included in the care of the woman as she desires. • The clinic is organized with the convenience, comfort and needs of the clients in mind. • The layout of the clinic includes a private place for examination and counselling, ensuring that discussion between care giver and pregnant woman cannot be overheard.

• The woman’s resources and capabilities are taken into consideration when health messages are given, and recommendations made. • The woman is informed about her health situation and good health practices. Her consent is sought in all decisions relating to her health and care. • The pregnant woman and her family are supported to become active participants in her care, particularly in making decisions. • She is assisted to overcome problems or challenges that impede her well-being.

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Compare your explanations on “person-centred care” to those listed earlier by participants and clarify/correct any differences. In giving feedback, also emphasize the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Care providers should make all pregnant women feel welcome at their clinic.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The opening hours of clinics providing ANC services should be as convenient as possible

for women visiting the clinic. (It has been shown that the number of women seeking antenatal care at health facilities increases proportionally with increases in ANC clinic hours of operation).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Health care providers should make every effort to keep their appointments with women in

order to reduce patient waiting time. However, women who come without an appointment should not be turned away even when there is no emergency.

Good rapport and communication between the care provider and the pregnant woman

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

Individualized care requires that, to the extent possible, the ANC provider establishes a unique relationship with their client, and at each visit, assumes responsibility for all decisions taken in respect of the client’s care. This implies that the care provider should have all necessary information (including sensitive information) about the woman to enable good decisions about her care. Obtaining such information requires good rapport and communication with the client.

To foster a good relationship with the client, the ANC provider must:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; know and appreciate the cultural milieu from which the client originates and must provide care within that

cultural context;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; provide a respectful and positive environment to the family member accompanying the pregnant woman;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; maintain privacy and confidentiality during all contacts with a pregnant woman; and

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; have good communication skills (which are essential to building rapport and effective care) and communicate in

a respectful, individualized and person-centred way.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 13

Slide 16: Good communication Explain that all women want a positive pregnancy experience from ANC. This means effective social, cultural, emotional and psychological support is provided in a respectful way to improve a woman’s perception of her pregnancy and her care.

Present slide 16 and highlight the benefits of good communication in supporting a positive pregnancy experience.

You may also use the additional information below to enrich the discussions.

Effects of culture on communication with clients:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Effective communication makes the woman (and her companion) feel welcome. It also makes it

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

easier to: elicit inputs from the woman about her symptoms or concerns; provide information about her needs; and help her understand her options and make decisions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain that the ANC provider must know and appreciate the cultural milieu from which their clients

originate and must provide care within that cultural context.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask participants to describe and discuss some of the cultural practices that are related to pregnancy

in their locality. Use examples of harmless and harmful cultural practices provided by participants to discuss how these could influence how you communicate and provide antenatal care services and whether women are comfortable using the services or not. For example, talking about genitalia in public may be culturally offensive. Women in such cultures may feel reluctant to discuss issues relating to vaginal discharge/ulcers (STIs).

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

Effect of clinic set-up/organization on communication with clients:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Highlight the fact that clinic set-up and service organization also affect communications between care

providers and clients significantly. Describe the “factory assembly care system” and its disadvantages in this regard.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; “The factory assembly line” approach of antenatal clinics is one in which several care providers are

responsible for the different care components. This usually results in poor-quality care, as none of the care providers along the line is in possession of all the information concerning the client to enable good decisions about the woman’s care. For example, when one provider takes blood pressure, another requests laboratory tests, and another provides counselling and preventive measures, good rapport is unlikely. The client is not managed wholly or comprehensively, certain problems are

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 7

overlooked, and her needs are therefore never entirely met.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask participants to share how ANC is conducted in their respective settings and discuss how this

affects the quality of care. Ask them a few checking questions to ensure that they understand what a factory assembly line approach to ANC is, as opposed to an individualized and comprehensive provider–client relationship.

Disease detection and not risk categorization

Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization Present slide 17 and explain that in past ANC models, information gathered during assessments of pregnant women was used to classify pregnant women by risk category to determine their chances of complications and the level of care required.

### 12 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 8

However, evidence has shown that all pregnant women remain at risk of complications. Many women with risk factors may not develop complications, while women without risk factors may develop and die from complications.

## 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain that risk assessment is good for planning management, but not for predicting outcome.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Antenatal care should be provided in a simple form that ensures that women with disease or special

health conditions and/or those at risk of developing complications are identified early and referred to a higher level of care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The higher levels of care to which they are referred must have the expertise to deal with their specific

health care needs.

Evidence-based practices during antenatal care Antenatal care provided by skilled health care providers must include only care practices that are proven to promote the health and survival of mothers and babies.

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Slide 18: Evidence-based interventions for ANC Ask participants: What are the recommended evidence-based practices during antenatal care? Write their answers on the flip chart. Then present slide 18. Evidence-based interventions in antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience include: • Assessing a client’s birth preparedness and complication readiness; prevention of malaria through intermittent preventive treatment in pregnancy (IPTp); prevention

of mother-to-child transmission of HIV, HBV and syphilis: prevention of anaemia through daily oral iron and folic supplementation; early ultrasound scan to estimate gestational age and monitor foetal development; and involvement of the client’s partner or support person in the process of ANC and in preparation for childbirth.

Using the PCPNC guide to provide evidence-based care

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The WHO document titled Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care: a guide for essential practice (herein

called the “PCPNC guide”) provides evidence-based standards for the provision of high-quality pregnancy and childbirth care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inform and remind participants that it is the main reference document for evidence-based ANC practices.

During training sessions on management of identified antenatal problems, you will refer to and facilitate the use of the PCPNC guide and its simplified messages for client care and counselling.

Help participants familiarize themselves with the PCPNC manual by tasking them to each review the organization and contents of the manual for 15 minutes and then answer the following questions:

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

1. Which chapters in the manual concern antenatal care provision? 2. Which sections will provide information on evidence-based practices for management of anaemia during pregnancy? 3. On which page can evidence-based counselling components for developing a birth and emergency plan be found?

Birth preparedness and complication readiness planning: a unique evidence-based antenatal intervention

Most complications leading to maternal and newborn death and ill-health occur around the time of childbirth. These complications are worsened by delays in seeking or receiving care. Antenatal care emphasizes the importance of preparing well for childbirth and possible emergencies as an essential life-saving intervention for women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 15

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness Read aloud or present slide 19 The care provider assists the client and her family to prepare for childbirth by: identifying the place for childbirth (a facility with a skilled provider is recommended); and planning how to get there and what items to have in readiness. • The care provider assists the client and her family to prepare for childbirth by: identifying the place for childbirth (a facility with a skilled provider is

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

recommended ); and planning how to get there and what items to have in readiness. • The care provider prepares their client for emergencies by educating the client and her family to recognize danger signs and symptoms and to seek immediate help. • The care provider provides advice on: the facility to visit for emergency care; how to get there; the contact address and telephone number of the facility; and the need to identify a blood donor in case a transfusion is needed.

• These plans are documented in the woman’s birth and emergency plan.

Scheduling antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience (5 minutes)

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 7

Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts • Ask participants what the recommended schedule for routine antenatal care is. Listen to their answers then present slide 20. • For a positive pregnancy experience, it is recommended that all healthy pregnant women have a minimum of eight scheduled comprehensive antenatal contacts during pregnancy. Refer them also to Figure 1.1 in their manual. • The first contact between the pregnant woman and the ANC care provider should occur in the first trimester.

• For women who come early in their pregnancy, early initiation of ANC facilitates confirmation of the pregnancy and evaluation of whether the woman is in good health without complications – among other key interventions.

Figure 1.1: Schedule of routine antenatal contacts for a normal, healthy woman

- Dòng dữ liệu: Contact; Recommended schedule

As soon as the woman suspects she is pregnant;

### 14 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 8

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1st contact; during the ﬁrst trimester (preferably 8–12 weeks)

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2nd scheduled contact; 20 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3rd scheduled contact; 26 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4th scheduled contact; 30 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: 5th scheduled contact; 34 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: 6th scheduled contact; 36 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: 7th scheduled contact; 38 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: 8th scheduled contact; 40 weeks

## 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Explain that the above schedule is recommended for uncomplicated pregnancy. More frequent contacts or different schedules are needed if the woman has any problems or any complications that require treatment or additional monitoring or support, such as malaria or HIV.

Discuss challenges in implementing the eight ANC visit schedule using the information below: In many low-resource settings, some clients come for their first contact during their second trimester of pregnancy. In this case, the contact schedule must be modified to ensure that the client receives all the essential components of care before childbirth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For example, a client who first contacts the clinic in her sixth month of gestation (at 25 weeks) should

### 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

be assessed as comprehensively as possible at this initial visit. If she is found to be healthy, the standard contact schedule should be modified to ensure that she can receive all the essential care interventions before childbirth: two doses of tetanus toxoid and several doses of SP for intermittent preventive treatment for malaria during pregnancy (IPTp). During the last contact, the woman should be asked to return if she does not give birth by 41 weeks of gestation.

It is important to ensure that the quality of ANC is assured at each visit.

Class activity: Case study: principles of antenatal care (15 minutes) In this exercise, participants will assess whether the care provided by the ANC provider demonstrated the principles of antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience.

### 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

Slide 21: Case study • Using Slide 21, explain the purpose and instructions for the case study activity. • Refer participants to the relevant section in their manuals. • Present or ask a participant to read out the case (see below). • When all participants have completed the assignment, discuss the answers using the answer guide provided below.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 17

Case study: Mrs Q

### 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

Mrs Q is 17 years old. She has missed her menses for the past three months and thinks she is pregnant. This is her first pregnancy. She is well today and has no health complaints except early morning sickness. She and her mother in law live about 2 km from the clinic. She walked here today because she has no money for transportation. Her husband, who recently lost his job as a labourer, has travelled to the big city to look for a new job, leaving her with his mother. Her mother in law, who

is a trader, has been giving her a bit of money every now and then to help out.

When the midwife examined her, she found that she looked healthy. Her uterus was just felt abdominally. The midwife’s impression is that Mrs Q is about 12 weeks pregnant.

She performed the following rapid tests and obtained the following results: urine dipstick = Normal; HIV rapid test = Negative; and syphilis rapid test = Negative.

### 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

The midwife also asked her to do some routine tests at the hospital laboratory. She obtained the following findings: Haemoglobin = 9 g/dl; sickling = negative; blood group = O RhD positive; stool exam = hookworm ova present; G6PD screen = negative

The midwife also informed Mrs. Q that she was healthy and would only require eight contacts during this pregnancy. Mrs. Q then received a routine supply of iron and folate tablets (one tablet each daily). The midwife then asked her to come for her next visit in one month, when she would be at 20 weeks’ gestation, along with her husband.

Now think about the answer to the following question.

Question: Were all the principles of antenatal care listed in the chart demonstrated during this visit?

### 16 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

In the answer sheet provided below, tick yes or no, and write what the midwife did or did not do that supports your answer. When everyone has completed the assignment, the class will discuss the answers.

Feedback discussions When participants have completed the assignment, discuss the answers as follows:

1. Ask for one or two or more volunteers to share their answers to each question. 2. Compare the answers received with the correct answers provided in the answer sheet below. 3. If there are differences, discuss the answers; clarify and ensure that participants understand the basis and principles for the correct answers.

## 18 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 18 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Answer Sheet

- Dòng dữ liệu: Principle; Yes No; Midwife’s action or omission

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1. Quality of care; X; The visit missed several tasks that the midwife

should have performed, so this was not a good quality visit. She did not undertake a comprehensive assessment, including taking an appropriate history at the ﬁrst visit; she did not provide counselling; she did not explain the eight- contact schedule.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2. Individualized, person-; X; She did not give treatment for anaemia (only gave

- Dòng dữ liệu: centred care; insuﬃcient

- Dòng dữ liệu: iron; supplementation), nor did she provide

counsel on nutrition and self-care; she did not ask if the husband would be able to come on her next visit, or if he would be in town.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3. Disease detection and not; X; She did not detect the woman’s anaemia; she did

- Dòng dữ liệu: risk categorization; not seem to have noticed that she had hookworm,

### 18 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

or perhaps she just did not mention it until she could give mebendazole in the second trimester.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4. Evidence-based practices; X; The visit did not cover assessing the client’s

- Dòng dữ liệu: for antenatal care; emergency preparedness and complication

readiness. It is early in the pregnancy, but the provider should have at least taught her emergency signs in pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 5. Birth preparedness and; X; This was not addressed at all, except perhaps in

- Dòng dữ liệu: complication readiness; trying to get the husband to come in. However, it

seems that it may be the mother-in-law who should be involved if the husband will be away.

Key messages

Slide 22: Key messages • To summarize and end the session, ask participants to read aloud the key messages. • Link each key message to the session objectives. • Allow questions from participants for clarifications. (5 minutes)

### 18 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 19

Summary and conclusions

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Antenatal care (ANC) seeks to improve quality of care and the well-being of pregnant women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For a normal healthy pregnancy, a minimum of eight comprehensive contacts are recommended at

around <12, 20, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38 and 40 weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; ANC emphasizes the following principles: quality of care, individualized care, disease detection and

not risk categorization, evidence-based practices, and birth preparedness and complication readiness.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A quality ANC contact is one which is comprehensive and individualized, meaning that the care

### 18 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

provider completes all the recommended assessments, detects and treats any complications, maintains a supportive relationship with the woman, develops or updates the birth and emergency plan, provides all appropriate preventive measures, and provides advice and counselling to meet the woman’s own medical and psychosocial needs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Person-centred care takes into consideration the cultural milieu of the client and uses good

communication skills. Clinic schedules and organization are designed to accommodate the comfort and convenience of clients and respect their rights to privacy and confidentiality.

## 20 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 20 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Session 2: Organizing antenatal care services • Reference manuals: PCPNC manual; WHO ANC recommendations (2016) • Site visit guide

Preparation • Gather training materials in advance • Go through the exercises, group work and drills to have a good understanding

Welcome and introduction

Slides 1 and 2: Welcome and session outlines • Present slide 1 to introduce the session and welcome participants to the session. • Using slide 2, present the outlines of the session

Aim and objectives of the session

### 20 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Slide 3: Aim and objectives of the session • Read out the objectives for this session • Explain that the organization of antenatal care must enable quality care at each contact by providing the space, privacy, equipment, supplies and medicines necessary for the provision of quality ANC. • Similarly, the set-up should also ensure a client- friendly environment, which is essential in the provision of quality ANC that will encourage the woman to continue to use the facility.

Requirements for client-friendly antenatal care services

Ask participants to list key requirements for the organization of antenatal care services and write their contributions on a flip chart, then present Slides 4, 5, 6 and 7

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 21

### 20 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

Slide 4 Slides 4–7: Client-friendly ANC • Ask participants to imagine being a client visiting the antenatal clinic and ask them to identify the various care areas within the clinic setting: waiting area, consulting room, etc. • Ask them to list features in those areas that promote a positive care experience for clients. • Write their contributions on a flip chart.

Present slides 4, 5 and 6 • Compare the answers provided by participants to the requirements listed in slides 4 to 6 • Discuss each listed requirement and its importance • Encourage participants to discuss some common clinic infrastructure and set-up challenges facing providers of antenatal services (as earlier listed by participants) in comparison to the recommended set-up requirements.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Referring to slide 7, remind participants of other key services that must be

linked to antenatal clinic services.

### 20 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Highlight how the negative effects of poor quality of care at these other

services can affect client experience and discuss how the quality of these other key requirements can be enhanced and linked to ANC.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask participants to reflect on the organization of antenatal care services in

their respective settings and how this impacts on the quality of the services they provide to their clients.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage trainees to brainstorm ways to improve upon clinic set-ups and

promote client-friendly antenatal services.

Workplace and administrative procedures (10 minutes)

Slides 8, 9 and 10: Workplace and administrative procedures Explain that the quality of ANC services hinges on application of workplace and administrative procedures.

Present slides 8, 9 and 10.

### 20 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk Substitutes at the health facility and its rationale. • The WHO International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk Substitutes was adopted in 1981 by the World Health Assembly to promote safe and adequate nutrition for infants, through the protection and promotion of breastfeeding, and by ensuring the proper use of breast-milk substitutes, when necessary.

## 22 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 22 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; One of the main principles of the Code is that health care facilities should

not be used for the purpose of promoting breast-milk substitutes, feeding bottles or teats. Subsequently, World Health Assembly resolutions have clarified the Code and addressed some related issues.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discuss adherence to the Code in their health facilities.

Ensure that the health facility is tobacco-free.

Infection prevention in ANC

Slide 11: Standard IPC practices for ANC To protect the woman, her baby and themselves from infection with bacteria and viruses, including HIV and HBV, health care providers must observe standard (also called universal) precautions when providing antenatal care as recommended in the PCPNC guide (section A4). • Discuss how care providers can promote adherence to hand washing for themselves and clients within their clinic settings.

### 22 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 23

Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Antenatal care provides a unique setting where the continuum of care is initiated during

pregnancy and continued long after the birth of the baby. Over the nine-month course of her pregnancy, a client makes several visits to the clinic where the progress of her pregnancy and her health status are checked and recorded.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The recorded findings at each visit provide the basis for judging a client’s well-being at the initial and

subsequent visits; and for planning her overall care. Clinical documentation is therefore a vital and very important component of antenatal care. It is also critical for the management, monitoring and evaluation of services provided and their quality.

### 22 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Emphasize that irrespective of the type of document in use, all notes or entries must be

timely, complete, legible and fully recorded. All clinical records and other documentation must be well maintained, stored and/or filed away properly.

Slide 12: Documentation and data management • Ask participants to list the various records utilized in the antenatal clinic during care provision and their uses. • Note their answers on a flip chart. • Compare their answers to those on slide 12 and fill in any gaps. • Describe the two types of client clinical records: home-based, an facility-based. • Using the information provided below, discuss the advantages and disadvantages of each.

WHO recommends home-based client records for maternal and child care.

### 22 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

Client clinical records Care provided must be recorded on a clinical record (electronic or paper-based) at the time the woman is seen, including ﬁndings, treatments, reasons for referral (if necessary), and follow-up recommendations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Home-based client records; Facility-based client records

- Dòng dữ liệu: Advantages; Advantages

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Available to client irrespective of clinic location or health; •; Documents may be

- Dòng dữ liệu: provider visited; kept more securely

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Available to client at time of labour; •; Full clinical records

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Improve client’s ownership and involvement in her own care; are available for later

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Can be a tool for behaviour change communication and patient-; review and/or

- Dòng dữ liệu: centred care; analysis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Improve communication between health providers and women

/caregivers.

### 22 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: Disadvantages; Disadvantages

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Records may be less securely kept with high risks of breach of; •; Retrieval of records

- Dòng dữ liệu: conﬁdentiality and poor maintenance (care should be taken when; may be a challenge in

- Dòng dữ liệu: recording conﬁdential information into home-based records if the; settings with poor

- Dòng dữ liệu: client does not want this).; storage and ﬁling.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Client clinical details may not be readily available for later review; •; Time spent on record

- Dòng dữ liệu: and data analysis.; retrieval

## 24 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 24 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Clinic registers and log books

These documents provide a log of clinic use by clients and are important for the recording of key care components received by clients at each visit. Where home-based client ANC books are used, they often become the only other available source of client clinical care information at each visit. Analysis of data from these registers allows evaluation of the coverage and quality of antenatal care services. They may be used to calculate the following monitoring and evaluation indicators:

### 24 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Slide 13: ANC indicators • Ask participants if they know of these indicators and use them in their settings. • Discuss how each of these indicators may be calculated (nominators and denominators) and their relevance and use in monitoring coverage and quality of care. • Refer participants to the relevant section in their manual. • Ask some participants to each read out a segment of the table dealing with the respective indicators.

Group antenatal care

Slides 14 and 15: Group antenatal care – organizational considerations • Conventionally, ANC service provision is organized in a manner that offers the pregnant woman an opportunity for a one-on-one consultation with her health care provider in most settings. • An alternate and newer approach to care known as group ANC (GANC) is practiced in some settings and has been shown to have some benefits. • Ask participants if they have heard of group ANC.

### 24 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

• Use the information provided on slides 14 and 15 to describe the aims, components, benefits and key considerations in the organization of group ANC.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 25

Establishing links for the continuum of care (10 minutes)

Quality ANC is provided in a continuum linking the family and community with the health facility. Support from communities and other health care providers can enhance the care provided to pregnant women.

Ask participants who they consider as key stakeholders and partners in caring for pregnant women.

Ask them to describe how they establish links with other health care providers, individuals within the community and community groups. List their responses on a flip chart.

Present and review the information on slides 16 and 17, comparing it with the information provided by the participants.

### 24 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care • Some antenatal care interventions can be offered through community-based care providers in a task sharing approach. • WHO recommends that the promotion of health-related behaviour change education and distribution of preventive interventions can be shifted where necessary to a broad range of cadres, including community-based lay health workers and auxiliary nurses (refer participants to the relevant appendix).

• Present the information on slide 16 to highlight important considerations that must be made during task shifting of these ANC components.

Slide 17: Establishing links for a continuum of care • Use the information on the slide to discuss how the other key stakeholders within the community can partner with health care providers in providing support to pregnant women clients to achieve a positive pregnancy experience.

Class activity 1: Brainstorming

### 24 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

Slide 18: Brainstorming activity • Describe the scenario of a pregnant woman visiting an antenatal clinic and then ask each participant in the class to give one answer to the question: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? • Each participant should mention one characteristic of an antenatal clinic set-up and service that promotes a positive antenatal experience for the client. • Compare their answers to the list given in the table below and fill in any gaps.

## 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

A positive antenatal clinic visit means:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clinic days are scheduled to ensure clients can utilize services conveniently.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A quick check is performed on all women attending the antenatal clinic.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The waiting area is clean and comfortable and accommodates clients and their support persons.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clients are seen promptly and do not have to wait for very long hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is auditory and visual privacy for antenatal clients.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is ready access to toilet facilities.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is clean drinking water and access to snacks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The consulting cubicles are kept clean, well-ventilated and free from clutter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The cubicles have seating for the client and her support person.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The consulting cubicles are arranged for good eye contact during counselling.

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clients are able to establish rapport with and see the same care provider at subsequent visits.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Necessary supplies and medicines are readily available in the cubicle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rapid tests are available in the consulting cubicle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The laboratory and pharmacy are easily accessed by antenatal clients.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ultrasonography services are available close to the clinic.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is an equipped emergency care treatment point nearby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The workplace is kept clean and orderly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Records of equipment, supplies, drugs and vaccines are kept up to date.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; All pregnant women are checked for anaemia and syphilis at their first antenatal visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; All pregnant women are checked for HIV status at their first visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; All women are counselled and offered HIV testing.

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A birth and emergency plan is developed with the woman at her first antenatal visit and reviewed at

each subsequent visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Preventive measures routinely provided to antenatal women include tetanus immunization, iron folate

supplementation, mebendazole, and intermittent preventive treatment for malaria.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women can ask questions and get advice about their discomforts and concerns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clients are advised and counselled on nutrition and self-care at each visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Findings and treatments are recorded on a clinical record and a home-based maternal record.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When clients leave the clinic, they know when they should return for their next visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 27

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

Class activity 2: Site visit/ review of an antenatal clinic set-up and practices

Site visits • A key objective of this session is to reinforce the key messages shared in Session 2, to enable participants to identify the key requirements for clinic set-ups and practices that promote a positive pregnancy experience at the antenatal clinic.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Participants will be tasked to: observe a selected clinic set-up and its practices, identify its strengths

and weakness, and make concrete and practical suggestions for transforming it into the ideal setting as discussed earlier in this session.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A facilitator should visit the selected clinic during the preparation phase of the training to: obtain

permission from the facility managers, familiarize himself/herself with the set-up, and inform care providers and managers of the facility of the visit schedule.

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Site visits should take place when the antenatal clinic is open and clients are available and being

attended to. This may require organizing the visit at a convenient time period in consultation with the facility managers.

Instructions for site visits • Present slides 19 and 20 on the objective and guidelines for site visits.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide participants with the tools for site review.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize participants into groups of 4–6 and assign each group a specific area to observe within the

clinic set-up (for example, waiting area, consulting cubicle, documentation, emergency treatment area, and other linked services such as laboratory, pharmacy, infection prevention and access to

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; washrooms). Each group must identify a leader and someone to take notes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assign each group a facilitator to supervise the group activity.

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow each group 30–45 minutes to conduct their observations and prepare their reports.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the site visits, participants should return to the classroom to report their findings, after which an

overall debriefing session will be conducted.

Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic • Introduce this exercise by saying that it can be a fun exercise but will also cause participants to think critically about their own clinic setting, its set-up, and the way that antenatal care is provided. • They will have to compare that to all that they have learned so far about the optimal clinic organization for providing focused antenatal care. • Ask them to be honest with their answers.

### 26 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 7

Slide 20: Guidelines for site visits: • Using this slide, carefully explain to participants the rule governing site visits, emphasizing the importance of showing the outmost respect for all staff and clients met at the site.

## 28 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 28 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Site visit guide and checklist

Date of visit:

Name of site:

Name of participant:

Site visits are learning trips generally made to observe procedures or practices at a particular site. The general principles guiding such trips include the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Know the purpose and objectives of the visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe all rules and guidelines for the visit. For example, keep only to intended sites of the visit; keep to

stated time schedules; obtain permission before operating equipment or managing patients at the site.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask questions to clarify issues or observations you are not clear about.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note your observations on the forms provided for later discussion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Show outmost respect and be polite to all staff and clients you meet at the site.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid criticizing and making disparaging comments about the staff and/or facility.

### 28 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Seek the permission of your facilitator and/or tour guide before undertaking any activities outside of the

stipulated objectives.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If during the visit to a health facility you encounter a life-threatening emergency, provide first aid if able and

immediately hand over the client to facility staff as soon as they are available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discuss your comments and observations at the debriefing and feedback session after the trip.

Goals and objectives of site visit: You will be informed by your facilitator of the speciﬁc goals and objectives of this site visit. Note these in the space provided below and use them to guide your observations during this site visit.

1. 2. 3.
