Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment

### 180 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

Getting ready for a visit 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and equipment are available: • sheet or drape • tape measure • foetal stethoscope • gloves • rapid test kits: HIV, syphilis • urine sample container and dipsticks • blood pressure machine • 0.5% chlorine solution • leak-proof container or plastic bag 2. Use an antiseptic hand rub or wash your hands thoroughly with soap and water and dry with a clean dry cloth or allow to air dry. 3. Welcome the client

### 180 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

• Welcome and receive the client and support person promptly and respectfully. • Perform quick check – if there is an emergency, conduct rapid assessment and management. If stable, continue with ANC. • Introduce yourself (if meeting client for the ﬁrst time). • Encourage support person to stay with client and oﬀer them seats. • Ask the woman why she has come to the clinic. • Explain all procedures and ask permission before any examinations or tests.

• Always keep the woman informed of what you are doing. • Be respectful and ensure privacy during history, examination and counselling. 4. Encourage the woman and her support person to ask questions; and listen to what they have to say.

### 180 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

History Note: Be ﬂexible with the order in which the questions are asked. For example, it may be better to link some questions with speciﬁc aspects of the woman’s physical examination. Allow the learners to ask appropriate follow-up questions while taking the ANC history and physical examination as needed.

Personal information (first visit) 1. Ask the woman’s name, age, address, and phone number (if available). 2. Ask if she has access to reliable transportation. 3. Ask what her/her family’s sources of income/ﬁnancial support are. 4. Ask if she is having a medical, obstetric, social, or personal problem or other concern, and if she has had any problems during this pregnancy. 5. Ask if she has received care from another caregiver during this pregnancy.

## 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: Continued...; Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

Menstrual and contraceptive history (first visit) 6. Ask about the ﬁrst day of her last menstrual period and about her menstrual cycles. Calculate her present gestation and EDC.

7. Ask how many more children she plans to have. 8. Ask if she has used a family planning method before. If she has, ask which method(s) and whether she liked the method(s). 9. Ask if she plans to use family planning after this baby is born. If so, ask which method. Present pregnancy (first visit) 10. Ask if she has felt the baby move. If she has, ask the woman when the baby ﬁrst moved and whether she has felt it move in the last day. 11. Ask what her feelings are about this pregnancy.

### 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

12. Ask how her partner and family feel about this pregnancy. Obstetric history (first visit) 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if she has had any of these problems during a previous pregnancy or during/following childbirth: • convulsions during pregnancy or during/after childbirth • caesarean section, uterine rupture, or uterine surgery during childbirth • tears through the sphincter and/or rectum during childbirth • postpartum haemorrhage

### 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

• stillbirth, preterm, low-birth-weight babies; babies who died before 1 month of age; three or more spontaneous abortions. 14. Ask if she has breastfed before. If yes, ask how long she breastfed and whether she had any problems. Medical and medication history (first visit) 15. Has she ever been diagnosed with any chronic illnesses, such as sickle cell anaemia, diabetes, hypertension, asthma, tuberculosis, syphilis, HIV, heart disease, etc.? 16. Ask if she has any allergies.

17. Ask if she has ever been in the hospital or had surgery. 18. Ask if she is taking any drugs/medications (including traditional/local preparations, herbal remedies, over-the-counter drugs, vitamins, or dietary supplements). Ask if she is using any bleaching creams. 19. Ask if she has received the complete series of ﬁve tetanus immunizations (TT / Td). If she has, ask if it has been less than 10 years since her last booster.

### 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 183

Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

Daily habits and lifestyle (first visit)

20. Ask if she works outside the home. 21. Ask if she walks long distances, carries heavy loads or does heavy physical labour. 22. Ask if she gets enough sleep and rest. 23. Ask what she normally eats in a day, and what she has eaten in the past two days. Ask if she eats any non-food substances such as dirt or clay. 24. Has she had a baby within the last year? If so, ask if she is currently breastfeeding. 25. Ask if she smokes, drinks alcohol, or uses any other possibly harmful substances.

### 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

26. Ask who she lives with. 27. Ask if: • anyone has ever prevented her from seeing family or friends, stopped her from leaving her house, or threatened her life; • she has ever been injured, hit, or forced to have sex by someone; or • she is frightened of anyone.

Interim history (return visits) Remember that questions about her present pregnancy should be asked at every ANC visit. 28. Ask if she is having a medical, obstetric, social, or personal problem or other concern. 29. Ask if she has had any problems or signiﬁcant changes since her last visit. 30. Ask if she has received care from another caregiver since her last visit. If so, ask who provided the care, what care was provided, and what the outcome of the care was.

### 182 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

31. Ask if any of her personal information has changed since her last visit. 32. Ask if her daily habits or lifestyle (workload, rest and dietary intake) changed since her last visit. 33. Ask if there has been a change in her medical history since her last visit. 34. Ask if she has taken the drugs/medications prescribed and followed the advice/recommendations provided at her last visit. 35. Ask if she has had any reactions to or side-eﬀects from immunizations or

drugs/medications given at her last visit.

## 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

Physical examination

Assessment of general well-being and blood pressure (every visit)

36. Observe her general well-being: • Her gait and movements (walks steadily and without a limp) • Her facial expression (is alert and responsive) • Her general cleanliness (no visible dirt, no odour) • Her skin (free from lesions and bruises) • Her conjunctiva (are pink, not white or very pale pink in colour) 37. Measure her blood pressure while the woman is seated or lying down with the knees slightly bent and relaxed: • If diastolic BP is >90 mmHg, ask the woman if she has severe headache,

blurred vision or epigastric pain, and check her urine for protein.

Preparing for further examination

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

38. Explain the steps in the physical examination and obtain the woman’s consent. 39. Ask her to empty her bladder. Have her provide a urine sample for urine testing. 40. Have the woman undress in private. Ask her to remove only enough clothing to complete the examination. 41. Provide her with a cloth to cover the parts of her body that are not being examined. 42. Help her onto the examination surface and assist her to a comfortable position.

Visual breast examination (first visit/as needed)

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): • Ask the woman to sit on the examination surface, uncover her body from the waist up, and place her arms at her sides. • Visually inspect the overall appearance of the woman’s breasts (contours, skin, nipples and note any abnormalities) • If nipples appear inverted, test for protractility by placing the thumb and ﬁngers on either side of the areola and gently squeezing • If the nipple goes in, it is inverted.

Abdominal examination (every visit)

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

44. Ask the woman to lieon her back with her knees slightly bent and uncover her abdomen. 45. Check her abdomen for scars: If there is a scar, ask whether it is from a caesarean section or other uterine surgery. 46. Measure fundal height: • If 12–22 weeks, palpate and estimate the weeks of gestation by determining the distance between the top of the fundus and the symphysis pubis. • If more than 22 weeks, use a tape measure to determine the

number of centimetres from the upper edge of the symphysis pubis to the top of the fundus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 185

- Dòng dữ liệu: Continued...; Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

47. Carry out fundal palpation: • Stand at the woman’s side, facing her head. • Place both hands on the sides of the fundus at the top of the abdomen. • Using the ﬂat part of your ﬁngers, apply gentle but ﬁrm pressure to assess consistency and mobility of the foetal part. 48. Carry out lateral palpation: • Move your hands smoothly down sides of uterus to feel for foetal back. • Keep the dominant hand steady against the side of the uterus, while

using the palm of the other hand to apply gentle but deep pressure to explore the opposite side of the uterus. • Repeat the procedure on the other side of the uterus. 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: • Turn and face the woman’s feet. • Place your hands on either side of the uterus with your palms below the level of the umbilicus and your ﬁngers pointing to the symphysis pubis. • Grasp the foetal part snugly between the hands to feel shape, size, consistency and mobility.

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 6

• Observe the woman’s face for signs of pain/tenderness during palpation. 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: • Place the foetal stethoscope (after 20 weeks) or Doppler on the woman’s abdomen (on the same side that you palpated the foetal back). • If using a foetal stethoscope, place your ear in close, ﬁrm contact with the stethoscope. • Remove your hands from the foetal stethoscope and listen to the foetal heart for a full minute, counting beats while watching the second hand of a clock.

• Feel the woman’s pulse at the wrist, simultaneously, to ensure that foetal heart tones, and not maternal pulse, are being measured.

Genital examination (first visit/as needed)

### 184 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 7

51. Tell the woman what you are going to do before each step of the examination. 52. Ask the woman to uncover her genital area and cover or drape her to preserve privacy and modesty. 53. Ask the woman to separate her legs while continuing to keep her knees slightly bent. Turn on and direct the light toward the genital area. 54. Use an antiseptic hand rub or wash your hands thoroughly with soap and water and dry with a clean, dry cloth or allow to air dry.

55. Put new examination or high-level disinfected gloves on both hands. 56. Touch the inside of the woman’s thigh before touching genital area.

## 186 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 186 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: Continued...; Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

57. Separate the labia majora with two ﬁngers, check the labia minora, the clitoris, the urethral opening, and the vaginal opening. 58. Palpate the labia minora: • Look for swelling, discharge, tenderness, ulcers, and ﬁstulas. • Feel for irregularities and nodules. 59. Check Skene’s gland for discharge and tenderness: • With your palm facing upward, insert your index ﬁnger into the vagina and gently push upward against the urethra and milk gland on each side, and then directly, on the urethra.

### 186 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

60. Check the Bartholin’s glands for discharge and tenderness: • Insert your index ﬁnger into the vagina at the lower edge of the opening and feel at the base of each labia majora. • Using your ﬁnger and thumb, palpate each side for swelling or tenderness. 61. Check the perineum for scars, lesions, inﬂammation or cracks in skin. After the examination 62. Immerse both gloved hands in a 0.5% chlorine solution: • Remove the gloves by turning them inside out.

• If disposing of the gloves, place them in a leak-proof container or plastic bag. • If reusing them, submerge them in a 0.5% chlorine solution for 10 minutes to decontaminate. 63. Use an antiseptic hand rub or wash your hands thoroughly with soap and water and dry with a clean, dry cloth or allow to air dry. 64. Help the woman oﬀ the examination table. 65. Share your ﬁndings with the woman.

### 186 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 187

Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

Testing

66. Perform a haemoglobin test (at contact 1 (<12 weeks), contact 3 (26 weeks) and contact 6 (36 weeks), and as needed based on signs and symptoms): • If the haemoglobin level is less than 7 g/dL, refer the woman to a hospital. • If the haemoglobin level is 7–11 g/dL, give iron/folate, 1 tablet twice daily. 67. Perform a syphilis screening test (ﬁrst contact): • If the result is positive, treat for syphilis with benzathine penicillin. • Plan to treat the newborn.

### 186 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

• Encourage the woman to bring her sexual partner for treatment. • Advise her on the correct and consistent use of condom to prevent re- infection after treatment. 68. Inform the woman that HIV testing is recommended for all pregnant women. • If she chooses to be tested, − conduct HIV testing using a rapid test kit, inform the client of the test result, and provide post-test counselling according to protocol; or − refer her to HIV testing services if not available at the clinic.

− All HIV-negative pregnant women should be retested at 36 weeks or according to national PMTCT guidelines. 69. Test for blood group and Rh on ﬁrst contact. If Rh negative, the woman is a candidate for anti-D immune globulin. 70. Test her urine for protein. 71. If the woman lives in an area with a high prevalence of diabetes /gestational diabetes, test her urine for glucose: • If the urine is positive for glucose, refer for diagnostic tests and treatment.

## 188 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 188 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

Care provision Note: Individualize the woman’s care by considering all ﬁndings gathered during assessment.

Advice and counselling

72. Provide advice and counselling about diet and nutrition: • Eat a balanced diet with a variety of foods. • Eat an extra serving of staple food per day. • Eat smaller, more frequent meals if necessary. • Take 1 iron/folate tablet daily. 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for normal birth and plans in case of emergency: • skilled provider and place of birth • transportation/emergency transportation and funds/emergency funds • decision-making and support person

### 188 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

• items for clean and safe birth and for the newborn • emergency signs during pregnancy and signs of labour. 74. Provide advice and counselling about common discomforts of pregnancy, as needed. 75. Provide advice and counselling about the use of potentially harmful substances: • smoking, drinking, alcohol, drugs/medications. • Inform the health worker before use of prescription drugs. 76. Provide advice and counselling about hygiene: • hand washing • safe drinking water • food handling/safety

### 188 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

• body, clothing, bedding, environment. 77. Provide advice and counselling about rest and activity: • decreasing heavy work • needing additional rest • periodic rest periods throughout the day • avoid lying on back • avoid standing for long periods • susceptibility to injuries • increased caloric needs. 78. Provide advice and counselling about sexual relations and safer sex: • abstinence or monogamy • use of condoms • other high-risk sexual practices • STIs

• change in sexual desire during pregnancy • intercourse not harming foetus • avoiding if ﬂuid leakage, bleeding, premature labour • changes in positions. 79. Provide advice and counselling about early and exclusive breastfeeding: • beneﬁts (economic, health for baby, health for mother, bonding) • general principles (beginning immediately at birth, beneﬁts of colostrum, exclusive breastfeeding, on demand breastfeeding).

### 188 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 189

- Dòng dữ liệu: Continued...; Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care Some of the following steps/tasks should be performed simultaneously

- Dòng dữ liệu: Step/task; Cases

80. Provide advice and counselling about family planning: • beneﬁts of optimum birth spacing (at least 3 years) • method choice for postpartum contraception • starting before fertility returns. 81. If the woman is HIV-positive, provide advice and counselling about HIV care. Counsel about ARV treatment and PMTCT. 82. Encourage the woman to ask questions and express concerns. 83. Ask the woman questions to be sure that she understands what is being said.

Immunizations and other prophylaxis

### 188 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

84. Give tetanus immunization (TT/Td) based on the woman’s history.

85. Give iron and folic acid for supplementation, enough tablets to last until the next visit, and counsel the woman about the following: • Eat food rich in vitamin C • Avoid tea, coﬀee, and colas • Possible side-eﬀects of medication. 86. Give intermittent preventive treatment for malaria with SP from 13 weeks gestation. 87. Give one dose of mebendazole after the ﬁrst trimester.

Other care

88. Provide treatment for illness (including malaria, syphilis, anaemia, and HIV infection). 89. Manage other problems identiﬁed.

Return visits

90. Schedule the next antenatal visit: • Make sure the woman knows when and where to come. • Write the date of visit in her take-home ANC card. • Answer any additional questions or concerns. 91. Thank the woman and her family for coming. 92. Record the ﬁndings of the assessment and care provided on the woman’s record.

Annex 6: Anti-malarial drug dosing/regimes for malaria in pregnancy

### 190 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 81

(Note: refer to national guidelines for treatment of malaria in pregnancy if available).

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; Uncomplicated malaria

Artemisinin-based oral combination therapy (ACT), such as artemisinin-lumefantrine, artesunate-mefloquine and artesunate-amodiaquine combination, are recommended for use only in the second or third trimester of pregnancy.

First trimester First episode:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Quinine 10 mg/kg booking body weight orally every eight hours, plus clindamycin 300 mg every six hours

for seven days. If clindamycin is not available, treat with quinine monotherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; An artemisinin-based combination therapy (ACT) can be used if quinine is not available, or if quinine plus

clindamycin fails, or if adherence to seven-day treatment with quinine cannot be guaranteed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Symptomatic treatment: paracetamol 1 g three times a day for five days; antiemetic treatment may be given

### 190 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 82

if necessary.

Second trimester First episode Oral artemisinin-based combination therapy (ACT) is recommended. Based on national protocols and assuming a body weight of 50kg or more, treatment options and doses are:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; artemether (80 mg) plus lumefantrine (480 mg) twice daily for three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; artesunate (200 mg) plus amodiaquine (540 mg) once daily for three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; artesunate (200 mg) plus mefloquine (440 mg) once daily for three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; dihydroartemisinin (160mg) plus piperaquine (1280 mg) once daily for three days; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; artesunate (200 mg) once daily for three days, plus sulfadoxine/pyrimethamine (1500/75 mg) single dose

only on day one.

Note: Quinine is associated with an increased risk of hypoglycaemia in late pregnancy. It should be used in combination with clindamycin only if effective alternatives are not available.

### 190 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 83

Subsequent episodes: Artesunate + clindamycin or quinine with clindamycin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; Severe/complicated malaria

Pregnant women with severe malaria are prone to hypoglycaemia, pulmonary oedema, anaemia and coma. Foetal death and premature labour are common.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; They should be managed at a CEmONC facility.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Parenteral antimalarial drugs should be given in full doses without delay.

All trimesters Parenteral artesunate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; is the treatment of choice in all trimesters. It causes more rapid parasitic depletion and does not cause

hypoglycaemia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Loading dose: artesunate 2.4 mg/kg body weight IV/IM every 12 hours for at least 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintenance dose: artesunate 1.2 mg/kg body weight IV as a single bolus once daily beginning on the

second day of treatment.

### 190 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 84

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue maintenance dosing until the woman is conscious and /or able to swallow; then give a complete

oral treatment with ACT for three days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 205

Or Parenteral quinine: should be given either IV or IM until the patient can swallow.

IV administration

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Quinine hydrochloride, 10 mg/kg body weight (max. 600 mg) IV 8 hourly in 5-10 ml/kg of 5% dextrose

over four hours; repeat 8 hourly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If required for over 48 hours, reduce the dose to 5-7 mg/kg to avoid toxicity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Change to oral medication when patient can swallow: oral quinine with clindamycin tablets for seven days

or ACT for three days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If parenteral artesunate or quinine is not available, give intramuscular artemether if the patient is not able

to swallow.

### 190 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 85

Note: If at BEmONC, give the initial dose of quinine IM over the anterior aspect of the thigh and refer to a CEmONC facility.

Other supportive measures

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; intravenous line with fluids, preferably 5% dextrose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; bladder catheter

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; oxygen

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; antipyretics (paracetamol is the drug of choice)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; anticonvulsive medication if convulsions occur

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; blood transfusion if Hb <7 gm/dL

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; treatment of hypoglycaemia.

Annex 8: Clinical and laboratory staging of HIV/AIDS for adults and adolescents

- Dòng dữ liệu: Stages; Conditions, signs and symptoms

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical stage 1; Asymptomatic; persistent generalized lymphadenopathy (PGL)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical stage 2; •; moderate unexplained weight loss (<10% of presumed or measured body

weight)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; recurrent respiratory tract infections (sinusitis, bronchitis, otitis media,

pharyngitis)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; angular cheilitis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; herpes zoster

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; recurrent oral ulcerations

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; papular pruritic eruptions

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; seborrhoeic dermatitis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; fungal nail infections of ﬁngers.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical stage 3; Conditions where a presumptive diagnosis can be made based on clinical

signs or simple investigations:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; severe weight loss (>10% of presumed or measured body weight)

### 208 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; unexplained chronic diarrhoea for more than one month

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; unexplained persistent fever (intermittent or constant for longer than one

month)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; oral candidiasis, oral hairy leukoplakia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; pulmonary tuberculosis (TB) diagnosed in the last two years

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; severe presumed bacterial infections (pneumonia, empyema, pyomyositis,

bone or joint infections, meningitis, bacteraemia)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; acute necrotizing ulcerative stomatitis, gingivitis, or periodontitis.

Conditions where conﬁrmatory diagnostic testing is necessary

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; unexplained anaemia and/or neutropenia (<500/mm3) and/or

thrombocytopenia (<50 000/mm3) for more than one month.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical stage 4; Conditions where a presumptive diagnosis can be made on the basis of

clinical signs or simple investigations:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; HIV wasting syndrome

### 208 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; recurrent or severe radiological bacterial pneumonia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; chronic herpes simplex infection (or labial, genital or anorectal of more

than one-month duration)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; oesophageal candidiasis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; extrapulmonary tuberculosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Kaposi sarcoma

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; central nervous system toxoplasmosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; HIV encephalopathy

Conditions where conﬁrmatory testing is necessary:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; extrapulmonary cryptococcus, including meningitis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; disseminated non-TB mycobacterial infections

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; progressive multifocal leukoencephalopathy (PML)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; candida of trachea, bronchi or lungs

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; cryptosporidiosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; isosporiasis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; visceral herpes simplex infection

## 210 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 210 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Annex 9: Skills practice – obstetric assessment of the abdomen for foetal growth and well-being (optional)

Learning guide

This guide describes the steps and tasks that a care provider should employ to examine the abdomen of a pregnant client at an antenatal contact. It is specifically designed to assess foetal growth and well-being and identify any obstetric risk conditions. The guide includes steps for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: A.; assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: B.; determining foetal lie and presentation.

A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height

The two techniques recommended for assessing uterine size and foetal growth are:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; measurement of symphysiofundal height with a measuring tape (for gestations greater than 22 weeks);

and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; assessment of fundal height by palpation using abdominal landmarks.

### 210 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; Measurement of symphysiofundal height (for gestations of 22 weeks or more) using a tape measure.

Steps to check the fundal height with a tape measure are as follows:

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; Place the zero line of the tape measure on the upper edge of the

symphysis pubis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; Stretch the tape measure across the contour of the abdomen to the

top of the fundus (located by palpation) using the abdominal midline as the line of measurement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.; Compare the reading on the tape measure to the gestational age by

dates (if known) to determine if foetal growth is satisfactory.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4.; Alternatively, place the zero line of the tape measure at the top of

the fundus and stretch it to the upper edge of the

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; Steps for estimating fundal height in weeks through palpation

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; Gently palpate the abdomen above the symphysis pubis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; Estimate the weeks of gestation by determining the distance

between the top of the fundus and the symphysis pubis or umbilicus, as shown in the diagram below:

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.; Compare the estimated gestational size in weeks to the gestational

age by dates (if known) to determine if foetal growth is satisfactory.

Annex 11A : Pre-test (without answers)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Questions; True; False

### part is palpable If the — phần 17

1. For a normal healthy pregnancy, a minimum of eight comprehensive contacts are recommended, preferably at: 8–20, 24, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38 and 40 weeks. 2. Complete periodic reports on births, deaths and other indicators according to regulations are one of the administrative activities that should be conducted either daily and /or occasionally to ensure the proper running of antenatal clinics. 3. There is no need to conduct a quick check for all women attending ANC services.

### part is palpable If the — phần 18

4. Sociocultural and religious beliefs that are harmful during pregnancy are not individual risk factors during pregnancy. 5. The clinical decision-making process should be continuous and ongoing. 6. The ﬁrst step in clinical decision-making is identifying the problem(s). 7. Clinical decision-making occurs before development of the care plan and does not need to occur again for that patient or client. 8. At the antenatal clinic, all pregnant women should be screened for

### part is palpable If the — phần 19

anaemia at every antenatal visit. 9. When checking for anaemia, the health care provider needs to ask the pregnant woman if she has headache. 10. The standard supplementary dose of iron and folic acid during pregnancy is: iron 180 mg (three tablets) daily + folic acid 400 mcg daily. 11. Mebendazole can be given in the ﬁrst trimester of pregnancy. 12. Group counselling is the best approach as it saves time for the provider; information reaches all members of the audience at the same time; and

### part is palpable If the — phần 20

it allows for experience sharing among pregnant women. 13. Developing a birth and emergency plan is important but not a key component of ANC. 14. Malaria can aﬀect both the mother and foetus, although complications in the foetus or newborn are rare. 15. Malaria is less severe in women during their ﬁrst or second pregnancies than it is in subsequent pregnancies. 16. Women who have HIV have an increased risk for severe malaria. 17. Anaemia in pregnancy is diagnosed when a woman’s haemoglobin

### part is palpable If the — phần 21

concentration is found to be less than 8 g/dL during the ﬁrst trimester or lower than 7 g/dL during the second trimester. 18. Counselling and testing for HIV, hepatitis B and syphilis (2) can also be oﬀered during labour and the postpartum period. 19. Foetal position/lie and placental position/maturity are common indications of ultrasound scanning during the ﬁrst trimester of pregnancy. 20. It is not necessary to have other clinical information to interpret ultrasonography reports.

## 218 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 218 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Annex 12B: Classroom quiz – mid course – sessions 1–6 (answers)

The following quiz can be conducted as a classroom question-answer activity. Simply read each question to the class and allow a show of hands for the response. If an incorrect response is given, provide the correct one. Avoid accepting answers from the same trainee(s). Encourage all trainees to participate, as the quiz provides you with an opportunity to assess their level of knowledge and understanding so far (for example, the quiz covers the content of sessions 1 through 5).

Depending on the progress made in this course, time constraints may prove a challenge. Suggested alternatives are: (i) reduce the number of selected questions asked; (ii) use some questions when summarizing the session as suggested in the previous session; and (3) hand out the quiz to the trainees as a take-home assignment, to be returned the next day for marking.

### 218 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

Present and discuss the correct answers as listed below.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Early detection and treatment of complications

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establishment of a supportive relationship between the woman and the health care provider

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Development of a birth and emergency plan with the woman

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provision of preventive measures

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provision of advice and counselling.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; What is the recommended schedule of antenatal contacts (that is, how many routine visits and at what

intervals)? Ans. Eight contacts at 12, 20, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38, 40 weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.; Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with the woman to maintain

privacy and confidentiality? Ans. Any five of the following:

### 218 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; ensure a private place for examination and counselling;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; ensure that discussions, especially about sensitive subjects, cannot be overheard;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; ensure that the woman has given her consent before discussing matters with her partner or family;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; never discuss confidential information about clients with other providers, or outside of the health

facility;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; organize the examination area so that, during examinations, the woman is protected (with a

curtain, screen or wall) from the view of other people;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; ensure all records are confidential and kept locked away; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; limit access to logbooks and registers to responsible health care providers only.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4.; What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to ensure the proper

running of antenatal clinics?

### 218 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Records of equipment, supplies, drugs and vaccines should be updated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Essential equipment should be checked daily to ensure proper functioning.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Staff lists and schedules should be established.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Periodic reports on births, deaths and other indicators should be completed according to

regulations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 5.; Why is it essential for health care providers to follow universal precautions when providing antenatal care?

To protect the woman, her baby, themselves and the community from infection with bacteria and viruses, including HIV.

### 222 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 12

treatments (DOTs). True of False treatments (DOTs). True of False True – she is treated with a combination of four drugs (RIPE): isoniazid, ethambutol, rifampicin and True – she is treated with a combination of four drugs (RIPE): isoniazid, ethambutol, rifampicin and pyrazinamide for at least six months, given as DOTs. pyrazinamide for at least six months, given as DOTs.

## 224 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 224 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: 15.; Name one drug that is recommended to treat a syphilis-positive pregnant woman?

Pregnant women should be tested for syphilis during the first trimester and those positive should be given a single dose of 2 400 000 IU of benzathine benzylpenicillin as soon as possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 16.; Name three antiretroviral medicines given to prevent mother-to-child transmission of HIV?

HIV-positive women should receive a single daily dose of TDF + 3TC (FTC) + EFV for life, and should be advised that the baby will also be given antiretrovirals (NVP or AZT) after birth and whilst breastfeeding. They will also be offered early infant diagnosis for HIV. (NB. Follow most up-to-date guidance as the ARV drug-related recommendations change frequently)

- Dòng dữ liệu: 17.; A baby born to an HBV-positive mother must be given hepatis B immunoglobulin and hepatitis B vaccine

### 224 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

at birth. True or false? True – all babies must now receive HB vaccine to protect them from infection irrespective of the mother’s status. Those with infected mothers additionally receive immunoglobulins to mop up any maternally transmitted infections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 18.; List five uses of ultrasonography in pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Estimation of the gestational age

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identification of whether the foetus is alive or not

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determination of the lie and position of the foetus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Detection of multiple foetuses

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identifying the location of the placenta

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determination of adequacy of liquor volume

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identification of uterine tumours that may obstruct childbirth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 19.; At which gestation should routine ultrasound be performed for all pregnant women according to WHO

### 224 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

recommendations? One ultrasound scan before 24 weeks of gestation (early ultrasound, best around 22–24 weeks) is recommended for all pregnant women, to estimate gestational age, improve detection of foetal anomalies and multiple pregnancies, reduce induction of labour for post-term pregnancy, and improve the woman’s pregnancy experience.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 20.; The ultrasound can be used to date the pregnancy most accurately and predict the expected date of

childbirth when performed in the third trimester. True or false? False

## 228 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### 228 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 1

Annex 14B: Post-test questions (answer sheet)

Select the best possible answer to each question.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; What is the recommended schedule of antenatal visits for a normal healthy pregnancy?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Four visits at 14, 24, 32 and 36 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: b); Monthly visits until 28 weeks, fortnightly until 36 weeks, then weekly until delivery

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); Eight contacts at 12, 20, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38, 40 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); As many visits as the patient wants

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; What should the health care provider do during all contacts with the woman to maintain privacy and

confidentiality?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Ensure a clean place for examination and counselling.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); Ensure that discussions, especially about sensitive subjects, cannot be overheard.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); Ensure that the woman has given her consent before discussing matters with her partner

or family.

### 228 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); Discuss information about clients with other providers.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.; Which one should be part of the daily administrative activities that should be conducted to ensure the proper

running of antenatal clinics?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Quantification and ordering equipment, supplies, drugs, and vaccines.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); Essential equipment should be checked to ensure proper functioning.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); Staff lists and schedules should be changed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); Monthly reports should be completed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4.; Which one of these is a standard precaution for infection prevention in antenatal care?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Always clean the abdomen with a disinfectant before palpation

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); Carefully recap all used needles before disposing of them

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); Use colour coded bins to disaggregate waste

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); Autoclave the Pinard every day

### 228 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: 5.; Which one of the following factors are individual risk factors during pregnancy?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); adolescent pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); low educational status

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); experiencing intimate partner abuse

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); All of the above

- Dòng dữ liệu: 6.; Which one of these describes community risk factors during pregnancy?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); great distance from a woman’s home to a health facility where the required care is available

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); lack of transportation between home and a health facility

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); Multiple pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); A and B above

- Dòng dữ liệu: 7.; The clinical decision-making process:

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); should be used only on contact 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); collects information in an organised fashion, saving time and resources

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); should be implemented repeatedly as different needs or problems arise

### 228 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); B and C above

- Dòng dữ liệu: 8.; The development of a care plan:

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); is based on the findings of the assessment

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); should be done as a group

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); is the responsibility of the care provider only

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); all of the above

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024; 229

- Dòng dữ liệu: 9.; Which one of these is part of the Quick check of an antenatal client?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Check whether the woman is being wheeled or carried into the clinic

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); Check if the woman has the ANC card

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); Identify women who have come with their husband and serve them quickly

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); Check if the woman has COVID test results.

### 228 Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: 10.; Which one of these is not an emergency sign for rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregnant woman?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Very difficult breathing, or central cyanosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); Weak and fast pulse.

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); White itchy vaginal discharge

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); Convulsions or unconsciousness.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 11.; When checking for anaemia, what does the health care provider need to ask the pregnant woman?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); If the woman tires easily and is breathless (short of breath)

- Dòng dữ liệu: (b); If the woman has experienced dizzy spells or light-headedness

- Dòng dữ liệu: (c); If she has been taking her iron supplementation

- Dòng dữ liệu: (d); All of the above

- Dòng dữ liệu: 12.; What is the dose of iron and folic acid to treat anaemia during pregnancy?

- Dòng dữ liệu: (a); Iron 60 mg (one tablet) + folic acid 400 mcg daily

- Dòng dữ liệu: (h); Give only to pregnant women who have never been dewormed before

The WHO Regional Office for Africa

The World Health Organization (WHO) is a specialized agency of the United Nations created in 1948 with the primary responsibility for international health matters and public health. The WHO Regional Office for Africa is one of the six regional offices throughout the world, each with its own programme geared to the particular health conditions of the Member States it serves.
